Overall ProgressThe inherited 5.8552s true disk-cold regression is traced to random evidence-to-source table reads. Final Overview cache-miss median/p95 is 0.148659/0.191350s, cached median/p95 0.010624/0.011001s, with query counts 33/1; final ML Governance smoke is 0.2619-0.2658s and warning-free.
Progress Typev5.35 is an additive local SQLite query-plan repair with portable PostgreSQL indexes. It changes no API payload, frontend behavior, detection/ML authority, Assistant behavior, or response safety.

T6. Decision

ATDR productization has a source-backed current-state lock, supervisor-template alignment, secure shell handoff, hardened read-only SOC Assistant behavior, Detection/ML contracts, SQLite-preserving persistence, durable background jobs, resumable ingestion, operational observability, PostgreSQL multi-worker coordination, deployment/recovery references, and controlled multi-source synthetic validation. GitHub Actions #50 validates the backend, frontend, and disposable PostgreSQL persistence/multi-worker workflows. The current local database was not reset or deleted. ATDR remains FastAPI + React + SQLAlchemy/Alembic, with SQLite for local development, optional PostgreSQL for shared deployment, local JWT/RBAC plus disabled-by-default MFU IAM, simulated analyst-approved response, and ML decision support only. The next evidence phases are provider-blinded/real-source Detection/ML validation and an approved-host rehearsal for shared storage, Linux services, TLS, monitoring, managed secrets, and measured recovery. Real-source hardware forwarding, production detection accuracy, completed provider-backed MFU IAM, real response enforcement, raw-log sharing with external LLMs, and production readiness remain unclaimed.

v3.90 Decision Update

v3.90 completes the next product architecture slice: an opt-in durable queue and separately launched worker for allowlisted long operations. It keeps the FastAPI startup command unchanged, does not launch itself automatically, protects staged input paths, scopes job access by role/owner, fails evidence-mutating work closed on expired leases, and keeps response/model/IAM/LLM actions outside the dispatcher. The next reliability step is not more worker features on SQLite; it is managed-worker supervision plus PostgreSQL/multi-worker validation on an approved shared-lab environment. Production readiness is still not claimed.

v3.92 Decision Update

v3.92 completes the local observability and single-worker supervision slice without adding an external service or schema migration. ATDR now distinguishes liveness from readiness, emits bounded metrics, rejects concurrent SQLite workers, records graceful worker stop state, surfaces operational warnings, and provides explicit protected audit retention. The next local productization step is resumable large-file ingestion and backpressure. PostgreSQL multi-worker, external monitoring/paging, and IAM preproduction validation remain separate environment-backed gates; production readiness is still not claimed.

The next phase is v3.94 PostgreSQL multi-worker runtime validation, managed worker deployment, shared staging design, and backup/restore concurrency drills on an approved host. Production readiness and global exactly-once ingestion are not claimed.
